## Corpus Check
- 4859 files · ~5,828,731 words

- Unclassified: 30 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 13, .css 4, .csv 3)

## Summary
- 54510 nodes · 172959 edges · 1534 communities (1195 shown, 339 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 88% EXTRACTED · 12% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 21163 edges (avg confidence: 0.94)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `d0a169a4`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
